##### 1. OsAlarm

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `18b4a4b7-e8da-49b4-b665-832209189ab9`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `OsAlarm`
- **多重性**: [0..32767]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

An OsAlarm may be used to asynchronously inform or activate a specific task. It is possible to start alarms automatically at system start-up depending on the application mode.

####### 5. OsAlarmScheduleEventRoundRobin

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `790cb604-ce45-4df5-a4ec-dd4c7215376b`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `OsAlarmScheduleEventRoundRobin`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container specifies, that the alarm is used for round robin scheduling.
Exactly one alarm with this container is needed per core which has one or more tasks that have round robin scheduling configured by means of the container: OsTaskRoundRobinScheduling.
Typically, this is enforced by a validator with a preferred solving action. So no need to add such an alarm manually.

####### 6. OsAlarmAutostart

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `b5905699-3386-4557-a4be-c332bc09a016`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `OsAlarmAutostart`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

If present this container defines if an alarm is started automatically at system start-up depending on the application mode.

######## 参数配置

########## 1. OsAlarmAlarmTime

- **UUID**: `d2ffa3fc-6499-48c6-9327-1aec8ddd46c0`

- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The relative or absolute tick value when the alarm expires for the first time. Note that for an alarm which is RELATIVE the value must be at bigger than 0.

########## 2. OsAlarmCycleTime

- **UUID**: `95284edd-aca6-4019-849e-81399d0e3673`

- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Cycle time of a cyclic alarm in ticks. If the value is 0 than the alarm is not cyclic.

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. OsAlarmAutostartType

##### 6. OsAlarmScheduleEventRoundRobin

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `790cb604-ce45-4df5-a4ec-dd4c7215376b`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `OsAlarmScheduleEventRoundRobin`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container specifies, that the alarm is used for round robin scheduling.
Exactly one alarm with this container is needed per core which has one or more tasks that have round robin scheduling configured by means of the container: OsTaskRoundRobinScheduling.
Typically, this is enforced by a validator with a preferred solving action. So no need to add such an alarm manually.

##### 7. OsAlarmAutostart

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `b5905699-3386-4557-a4be-c332bc09a016`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `OsAlarmAutostart`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

If present this container defines if an alarm is started automatically at system start-up depending on the application mode.

An AUTOSAR OS must be capable of supporting a collection of OS objects (tasks, interrupts, alarms, hooks etc.) that form a cohesive functional unit. This collection of objects is termed an OS-Application.

- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter enables the MICROSAR OS extension to the standard trusted function feature. Trusted function stubs package all user configured parameters and provide an API to call the trusted function with the specified parameters.

Syntax: Os_Call_<TrustedFunctionName>(arg1, arg2, ...)

true: Trusted function stubs will be generated
false: No stubs will be generated

######## 配置容器

######### 1. OsTrustedFunctionParam

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `cd055787-3be0-4495-8076-d61f0d1f5a86`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `OsTrustedFunctionParam`
- **多重性**: [0..127]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container includes all configuration sub-attributes of one trusted function parameter.
These will be used for trusted function stubs generation. This feature can be enabled with /MICROSAR/Os/OsApplication/OsAppUseTrustedFunctionStubs.
If no parameter is specified, 'void' is generated.

Note: Passed pointers have to be checked for validity by the trusted function implementation.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **3** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. OsTrustedFunctionParamId

- **UUID**: `438711f2-ad93-47a6-b518-b3ff78b05b07`
- **参数名**: `OsTrustedFunctionParamId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..127]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter is used to order the trusted function parameters.

########### STRING 类型参数

############ 1. OsTrustedFunctionParamDataType

- **UUID**: `d300f6cc-b177-4a9f-b043-a9e06255453a`
- **参数名**: `OsTrustedFunctionParamDataType`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **字符串变体**:
  - 默认值: `uint8`
- **描述**: This parameter defines the C data type of the trusted function parameter.

Note: If IN parameter is a complex datatype, it shall be passed by reference ('const <DataType>*').

########### ENUMERATION 类型参数

############ 1. OsTrustedFunctionParamDirection

- **UUID**: `96cc56c0-fc3a-43dc-9950-e9d8baf0dfc1`
- **参数名**: `OsTrustedFunctionParamDirection`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `IN`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `IN` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `OUT` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `IN_OUT` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter defines the usage direction of a trusted function parameter.

IN: Parameter is only used as input value (passed by value or reference: '<DataType> arg').
OUT: Parameter is only used as output value (always passed by reference: '<DataType> *arg').
IN_OUT: Parameter is used as input and output value (always passed by reference: '<DataType> *arg').

Where <DataType> is the configured data type string.

- **多重性**: [0..65534]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Container to structure the configuration parameters of MICROSAR OS non-trusted functions.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **3** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. OsNonTrustedFunctionStacks

- **UUID**: `ab03686c-9af6-453a-ade6-2219290286d0`
- **参数名**: `OsNonTrustedFunctionStacks`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..4294967295]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Specifies the maximum number of stacks for the non-trusted function.

Each parallel call of a non-trusted function needs its own stack. So this value must be equal to the maximum number of parallel calls of this function.

########## 2. OsNonTrustedFunctionStackSize

- **UUID**: `ad876b74-9e1c-44b1-9681-f15d879ae8c1`
- **参数名**: `OsNonTrustedFunctionStackSize`

- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1024`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This attribute specifies the stack size of the non-trusted function in bytes. In case of parallel calls each stack is set up with the same size.

###### 配置容器

本模块包含 **1** 个配置容器：

####### 1. OsTrustedFunctionParam

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `cd055787-3be0-4495-8076-d61f0d1f5a86`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `OsTrustedFunctionParam`
- **多重性**: [0..127]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container includes all configuration sub-attributes of one trusted function parameter.
These will be used for trusted function stubs generation. This feature can be enabled with /MICROSAR/Os/OsApplication/OsAppUseTrustedFunctionStubs.
If no parameter is specified, 'void' is generated.

Note: Passed pointers have to be checked for validity by the trusted function implementation.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **3** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. OsTrustedFunctionParamId

- **UUID**: `438711f2-ad93-47a6-b518-b3ff78b05b07`
- **参数名**: `OsTrustedFunctionParamId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..127]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter is used to order the trusted function parameters.

######### STRING 类型参数

########## 1. OsTrustedFunctionParamDataType

- **UUID**: `d300f6cc-b177-4a9f-b043-a9e06255453a`
- **参数名**: `OsTrustedFunctionParamDataType`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **字符串变体**:
  - 默认值: `uint8`
- **描述**: This parameter defines the C data type of the trusted function parameter.

Note: If IN parameter is a complex datatype, it shall be passed by reference ('const <DataType>*').

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. OsTrustedFunctionParamDirection

- **UUID**: `96cc56c0-fc3a-43dc-9950-e9d8baf0dfc1`
- **参数名**: `OsTrustedFunctionParamDirection`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `IN`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `IN` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `OUT` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `IN_OUT` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter defines the usage direction of a trusted function parameter.

IN: Parameter is only used as input value (passed by value or reference: '<DataType> arg').
OUT: Parameter is only used as output value (always passed by reference: '<DataType> *arg').
IN_OUT: Parameter is used as input and output value (always passed by reference: '<DataType> *arg').

Where <DataType> is the configured data type string.

##### 16. OsBarrier

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `5f999888-8afe-4834-b785-c1677a7196e0`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `OsBarrier`
- **多重性**: [0..65534]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

A barrier provides a method that synchronizes tasks. The calling task blocks until all participating tasks reach the barrier. Only one task per core may participate in a barrier.

此容器包含 **1** 个引用：

####### REFERENCE 类型引用

- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: References to OsTasks which are participating in the barrier.

##### 17. OsCore

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `95f5fc52-df0c-4646-99b2-03979dbec677`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `OsCore`
- **多重性**: [1..32]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container defines all configuration parameters for a MICROSAR OS-Core.

Each physical core shall be represented by an OS-Core.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **3** 个参数：

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. OsCoreIsAutostart

- **UUID**: `d68d3122-05d8-4dd6-ad77-a79672c9f2ea`
- **参数名**: `OsCoreIsAutostart`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Specifies whether the core has been already started before the OS initialization.

Note: This could be done by hardware or software (e.g. hypervisor or startup code). If in doubt use the proposed default value.

######## 2. OsCoreIsAutosar

- **UUID**: `5c00bfc7-5707-4bac-99e5-585d747c736f`
- **参数名**: `OsCoreIsAutosar`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Specifies whether the OS-Core is an AUTOSAR core or not. Which means this core is used with the MICROSAR OS.

####### STRING 类型参数

######## 1. OsCoreEntrySymbol

- **UUID**: `f0ce8480-8017-47ef-a1de-4a88aae87086`
- **参数名**: `OsCoreEntrySymbol`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the entry symbol (startup/reset vector) of the OS-Core. It shall be the linker label where the core starts on multi-core systems.

Note: If no default entry symbol is configured, this parameter can be omitted.

###### 引用关系

此容器包含 **8** 个引用：

####### REFERENCE 类型引用

######## 1. OsCoreIdleTask

- **UUID**: `e0314e34-e1b4-4ac3-82e6-7a11ec22898d`
- **引用名**: `OsCoreIdleTask`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **目标**: `OsTask`
- **完整路径**: `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/Os/OsTask`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: References which OsTask is used as idle task for the OS-Core.

The idle task runs if the core is in idle mode.
Each OS-Core needs his own idle task.

######## 2. OsCorePreStartTask

- **UUID**: `a841d029-4067-4f59-b772-6876d1d6d682`
- **引用名**: `OsCorePreStartTask`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **目标**: `OsTask`
- **完整路径**: `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/Os/OsTask`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Optional reference to an OsTask which shall be used as pre-start task for the OS-Core.

A pre-start task is necessary if MemoryProtection, peripheral region services, trusted function or non-trusted functions shall be used before StartOS().
Each OS-Core needs his own pre-start task.

######## 3. OsCoreSystemApplication

- **UUID**: `34682b0c-c5c8-4fb3-99ff-8a5af41a75e4`
- **引用名**: `OsCoreSystemApplication`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **目标**: `OsApplication`
- **完整路径**: `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/Os/OsApplication`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: References which OsApplication is used as system OS-Application for the OS-Core.

Each OS-Core needs his own system OS-Application.

######## 4. OsCoreResScheduler

- **UUID**: `4775f5a1-1a89-42e9-aaf0-67fcdb8eac7f`
- **引用名**: `OsCoreResScheduler`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **目标**: `OsResource`
- **完整路径**: `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/Os/OsResource`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter references the RES_SCHEDULER resource for this OS-Core.

Note: For multi-core systems the RES_SCHEDULER is generated for each core with name 'RES_SCHEDULER_<CoreName>'.

######## 5. OsCoreTimingProtectionCounter

- **UUID**: `e7f20e05-116e-4305-89a3-44d4ea551523`
- **引用名**: `OsCoreTimingProtectionCounter`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **目标**: `OsCounter`
- **完整路径**: `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/Os/OsCounter`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Optional reference to a high resolution OsCounter which is used for timing protection on this OS-Core.

Only necessary if timing protection shall be used.
Timing protection is only allowed if /MICROSAR/Os/OsOS/OsScalabilityClass is set to SC2 or SC4.

######## 6. OsCorePhysicalCoreRef

- **UUID**: `1ccd2c76-83d6-4a06-b456-aa177db586d1`
- **引用名**: `OsCorePhysicalCoreRef`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **目标**: `OsPhysicalCore`
- **完整路径**: `/MICROSAR/Os/OsPublishedInformation/OsDerivativeInformation/OsPhysicalCore`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Reference to the derivative specific definition of a hardware core. This reference is used to describe to which hardware core the OS-Core is bound.

######## 7. OsCoreRoundRobinSchedulingAlarm

- **UUID**: `a5abe2c1-b507-4f4f-9275-0ee7f5e377cd`
- **引用名**: `OsCoreRoundRobinSchedulingAlarm`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **目标**: `OsAlarm`
- **完整路径**: `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/Os/OsAlarm`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Optional reference to an alarm with the action OsAlarmScheduleEventRoundRobin. That alarm is used to trigger round robin scheduling events on this core.
The reference is needed if one or more tasks on the core have round robin scheduling configured by means of the container:  OsTaskRoundRobinScheduling.
The reference shall not be available if no task on the core has round robin scheduling configured.

####### SYMBOLIC-NAME-REFERENCE 类型引用

######## 1. OsCoreEcucCoreRef

- **UUID**: `79f9dcb0-d5cc-4c39-95d9-914b66298acc`
- **引用名**: `OsCoreEcucCoreRef`
- **引用类型**: SYMBOLIC-NAME-REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **目标**: `EcucCoreDefinition`
- **完整路径**: `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/EcuC/EcucHardware/EcucCoreDefinition`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Reference to the core definition in the Ecuc module where the CoreId is defined. This reference is used to describe to which core the OS-Core is bound.

To map an OS-Application to this OS-Core the corresponding OsApplication needs an OsApplicationCoreRef to the same EcucCoreDefinition in the Ecuc module.

####### 2. OsCoreXSignalChannel

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `8354f2bf-28a9-4113-86b4-1c7e1e7b97fa`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `OsCoreXSignalChannel`
- **多重性**: [0..31]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container structures all parameters for a cross-core API call channel. It shall be used to trigger APIs on another OS-Core.
Only one send channel per OS-Core is possible.

Valid APIs to be callable cross-core shall be defined in the corresponding receiver ISR (OsIsr/OsIsrXSignalReceiver).

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **1** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. OsCoreXSignalChannelSize

- **UUID**: `1c2d1e22-e0a9-4207-a946-a2ecf230a702`
- **参数名**: `OsCoreXSignalChannelSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..65534]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Specifies the number of cross-core service requests that can be queued.

##### 20. OsCounter

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `08db1d28-a89a-4373-8022-c852d3962b81`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `OsCounter`
- **多重性**: [0..255]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Configuration information for the counters that belong to the OS-Application.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **5** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. OsCounterMaxAllowedValue

- **UUID**: `413c948c-844b-4719-a852-4725efe6c4e6`
- **参数名**: `OsCounterMaxAllowedValue`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1073741823`
- **取值范围**: [1..4294967295]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Maximum allowed value of the system counter in ticks.

######## 2. OsCounterMinCycle

- **UUID**: `7e3f5dea-1b18-420a-ba78-1d3bb0403180`
- **参数名**: `OsCounterMinCycle`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..4294967295]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The MINCYCLE attribute specifies the minimum allowed number of counter ticks for a cyclic alarm linked to the counter.

######## 3. OsCounterTicksPerBase

- **UUID**: `ce2fd755-c378-43b5-a7e9-4c4e87d8e161`
- **参数名**: `OsCounterTicksPerBase`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..4294967295]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The TICKSPERBASE attribute specifies the number of ticks required to reach a counter specific unit. The interpretation is implementation-specific.
The value is calculated by dividing the desired counter period (OsSecondsPerTick) by the period of a single hardware tick, which is calculated by the reciprocal of the frequency (1/fosc).

####### FLOAT 类型参数

######## 1. OsSecondsPerTick

- **UUID**: `be4f34d2-2ce8-4a33-a0dd-040452be0731`
- **参数名**: `OsSecondsPerTick`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0.001`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Time of one counter tick in seconds.
The value specifies the desired tick period of the counter in seconds. (0.001 = 1ms)

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. OsCounterType

- **UUID**: `ef8e5a99-e1de-41c3-8c8e-251673d1e0d8`
- **参数名**: `OsCounterType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION

- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC

- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `HARDWARE` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `SOFTWARE` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **描述**: This parameter contains the natural type or unit of the counter.

**容器属性**:

- **容器名称**: `OsIocDataProperties`
- **多重性**: [1..127]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Data properties of the data to be transferred on the IOC communication channel.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **4** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. OsIocDataPropertyIndex

- **UUID**: `1f0fb155-ea36-41b3-89bb-f4bf3653a8f8`
- **参数名**: `OsIocDataPropertyIndex`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..126]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter is used to define in which order the data is send, e.g. whether IocSendGroup(A,B) or IocSendGroup(B,A) shall be used.

########### BOOLEAN 类型参数

############ 1. OsIocDataTypeIsAtomic

- **UUID**: `9210cdd0-404f-49bb-9da7-1dbb3c62f77d`
- **参数名**: `OsIocDataTypeIsAtomic`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Specifies if the type in OsIocDataType is an atomic data type or not. Which means it can be copied with one copy instruction and no locks are necessary.

########### STRING 类型参数

############ 1. OsIocInitValue

- **UUID**: `0c5ab3f8-247b-4eec-a81e-ade82fc8c983`
- **参数名**: `OsIocInitValue`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **字符串变体**:
  - 默认值: `0`
- **描述**: Initial Value for the data to be transferred on the IOC communication channel.

Note: If the used data is of a complex type the parameter has to define a name of a constant (not a value) which contains the initial value for the data. In case an intergral type is used the parameter may be both: a literal value or a name of a constant containing the value as above.

############ 2. OsIocDataType

- **UUID**: `291ac072-773f-459a-8223-2f0192460a35`
- **参数名**: `OsIocDataType`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **字符串变体**:
  - 默认值: `uint32`
- **描述**: This is the type of the data to be transferred on the IOC communication channel. This attribute is necessary to generate the parameter type of the IOC functions.

Currently this parameter is used instead of OsIocDataTypeRef to specify the data type.
These types are recognised as primitive:
All integer and float based types defined by AUTOSAR in StdTypes and PlatformTypes.
Additionally, also the public integer based types and enumerations defined in Os_Types.h and Os_Types_Lcfg.h are recognised.
These are:

AccessType
AlarmType
ApplicationStateType
ApplicationType
AppModeType
boolean
CoreIdType
CounterType
EventMaskType
float32
float64
IdleModeType
ISRType
MemorySizeType
ObjectAccessType
ObjectTypeType
OSServiceIdType
Os_BarrierIdType
Os_ErrorParmIndexType
Os_ExceptionSourceIdType
Os_FastTrustedFunctionIndexType
Os_InterruptSourceIdType 
Os_MemAccessType
Os_NonTrustedFunctionIndexType
Os_ObjectIdType 
Os_PeripheralIdType
Os_StatusType
Os_TickDiffType
Os_TraceSpinlockIdType
Os_TraceThreadIdType
PhysicalTimeType
ProtectionReturnType
ResourceType
RestartType
ScheduleTableStatusType
ScheduleTableType
sint8
sint8_least
sint16
sint16_least
sint32
sint32_least
sint64
SpinlockIdType
StatusType
Std_ReturnType
TaskStateType
TaskType
TickType
TrustedFunctionIndexType
TryToGetSpinlockType
uint8
uint8_least
uint16
uint16_least
uint32
uint32_least
uint64

All other types used as OsIocDataType are handled as reference and invoke memory copy actions which increase runtime in comparison.

########## 引用关系

此容器包含 **1** 个引用：

########### FOREIGN-REFERENCE 类型引用

- **引用名**: `OsIocDataTypeRef`

- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **目标类型**: IMPLEMENTATION-DATA-TYPE
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Not Used: This is the type of the data to be transferred on the IOC communication channel. This attribute is necessary to generate the parameter type of the Ioc functions. Additionally this information should be used to compute the data size for necessary data copy operations within the Ioc module.

  - `MACRO` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)

########## 2. OsIocDataType

- **UUID**: `291ac072-773f-459a-8223-2f0192460a35`

##### 25. OsIocCommunication

- **描述**: This attribute defines the size of the IOC internal queue to be allocated for a queued communication.

- **UUID**: `0c5ab3f8-247b-4eec-a81e-ade82fc8c983`
- **参数名**: `OsIocInitValue`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **字符串变体**:
  - 默认值: `0`
- **描述**: Initial Value for the data to be transferred on the IOC communication channel.

Note: If the used data is of a complex type the parameter has to define a name of a constant (not a value) which contains the initial value for the data. In case an intergral type is used the parameter may be both: a literal value or a name of a constant containing the value as above.

######## 2. OsIocDataType

- **UUID**: `291ac072-773f-459a-8223-2f0192460a35`
- **参数名**: `OsIocDataType`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **字符串变体**:
  - 默认值: `uint32`
- **描述**: This is the type of the data to be transferred on the IOC communication channel. This attribute is necessary to generate the parameter type of the IOC functions.

Currently this parameter is used instead of OsIocDataTypeRef to specify the data type.
These types are recognised as primitive:
All integer and float based types defined by AUTOSAR in StdTypes and PlatformTypes.
Additionally, also the public integer based types and enumerations defined in Os_Types.h and Os_Types_Lcfg.h are recognised.
These are:

AccessType
AlarmType
ApplicationStateType
ApplicationType
AppModeType
boolean
CoreIdType
CounterType
EventMaskType
float32
float64
IdleModeType
ISRType
MemorySizeType
ObjectAccessType
ObjectTypeType
OSServiceIdType
Os_BarrierIdType
Os_ErrorParmIndexType
Os_ExceptionSourceIdType
Os_FastTrustedFunctionIndexType
Os_InterruptSourceIdType 
Os_MemAccessType
Os_NonTrustedFunctionIndexType
Os_ObjectIdType 
Os_PeripheralIdType
Os_StatusType
Os_TickDiffType
Os_TraceSpinlockIdType
Os_TraceThreadIdType
PhysicalTimeType
ProtectionReturnType
ResourceType
RestartType
ScheduleTableStatusType
ScheduleTableType
sint8
sint8_least
sint16
sint16_least
sint32
sint32_least
sint64
SpinlockIdType
StatusType
Std_ReturnType
TaskStateType
TaskType
TickType
TrustedFunctionIndexType
TryToGetSpinlockType
uint8
uint8_least
uint16
uint16_least
uint32
uint32_least
uint64

All other types used as OsIocDataType are handled as reference and invoke memory copy actions which increase runtime in comparison.

此容器包含 **1** 个引用：

####### FOREIGN-REFERENCE 类型引用

######## 1. OsIocDataTypeRef

- **UUID**: `c1768e66-420a-45e4-b9f0-4fee0784773a`
- **引用名**: `OsIocDataTypeRef`
- **引用类型**: FOREIGN-REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **目标类型**: IMPLEMENTATION-DATA-TYPE
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Not Used: This is the type of the data to be transferred on the IOC communication channel. This attribute is necessary to generate the parameter type of the Ioc functions. Additionally this information should be used to compute the data size for necessary data copy operations within the Ioc module.

##### 29. OsIsr

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `1675c421-11c3-4f77-84db-b9868877ab34`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `OsIsr`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

The OsIsr container represents an interrupt service routine.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **10** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. OsIsrInterruptSource

- **UUID**: `3c81dd32-a905-479c-8099-5e4a3f492fe5`
- **参数名**: `OsIsrInterruptSource`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Specifies the interrupt source number of the ISR.

######## 2. OsIsrInterruptPriority

- **UUID**: `32a483a3-c5df-4ebe-91f6-ed1f394a4c8a`
- **参数名**: `OsIsrInterruptPriority`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The priority (level) of an ISR is defined by the value of this attribute. 

This value represents the hardware specific interrupt priority, so refer to hardware manual for ordering and number of maximum priorities.

######## 3. OsIsrStackSize

- **UUID**: `3f3e2357-76ab-4982-9b1f-2847e6830370`
- **参数名**: `OsIsrStackSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1024`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This attribute specifies the stack size of the Interrupt in bytes.

Interrupts with disabled nesting will share one stack per core.
Interrupts on the same level (priority) and enabled nesting will share one stack per level and core.
In both cases the biggest configured size will be used to set up the stack size.

Only applicable for category 2 ISRs. Category 1 ISRs will use the current active stack.

######## 4. OsIsrMemoryProtectionIdentifier

- **UUID**: `3a796f1c-d55f-40b1-be36-b0c50e4f2fbb`
- **参数名**: `OsIsrMemoryProtectionIdentifier`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This optional parameter can be used to specify a memory protection identifier for the ISR.

If this parameter is not set but the owner OS-Application has a memory protection identifier specified this value will be used for the interrupt.

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. OsIsrEnableNesting

- **UUID**: `9333680b-ed53-469d-a8f1-f19afc24cbda`
- **参数名**: `OsIsrEnableNesting`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Specifies whether the ISR is interruptible by another ISR or not.

true: Interrupts will be enabled before entering the interrupt handler function. So ISRs with higher priority can interrupt the ISR.
false: Interrupting of the ISR is only possible by FIQs, NMIs or exceptions (Depending on hardware).

######## 2. OsIsrUsesFpu

- **UUID**: `1fe0ca6b-fcae-4a26-b44a-c5558b9879c3`
- **参数名**: `OsIsrUsesFpu`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter is used to specify if this ISR uses the FPU unit.

This parameter only takes effect, if the parameter /MICROSAR/Os/OsOS/OsFpuUsage is set to "INDIVIDUAL".

######## 3. OsIsrInitialEnableInterruptSource

- **UUID**: `2df545ab-3b10-4f99-a9e2-c3bc351ce859`
- **参数名**: `OsIsrInitialEnableInterruptSource`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Specifies whether the interrupt source shall be enabled by a call to Os_InitialEnableInterruptSources().

true: Os_InitialEnableInterruptSources() will enable the interrupt source.
false: Os_InitialEnableInterruptSources() will ignore the interrupt source.

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. OsIsrCategory

- **UUID**: `559ea516-df31-49e9-8bb7-f32603b2fc14`
- **参数名**: `OsIsrCategory`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `CATEGORY_2`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `CATEGORY_0` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `CATEGORY_1` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `CATEGORY_2` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **描述**: This attribute specifies the category of this ISR.

######## 2. OsIsrInterruptType

- **UUID**: `0b5870d9-15aa-4b6a-80e2-3a37c07eb096`
- **参数名**: `OsIsrInterruptType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `EXTERNAL`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `EXTERNAL` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `EXCEPTION` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter specifies the interrupt source type of the ISR.

####### FUNCTION-NAME 类型参数

######## 1. OsIsrSpecialFunctionName

- **UUID**: `91a7d011-501e-4736-a5cb-84ff792a6263`
- **参数名**: `OsIsrSpecialFunctionName`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter can be used to specify an user defined function name which is called if the configured interrupt occurs. By default the function name is created by the container name.

This can be used if multiple interrupt sources shall share the same interrupt handler function.

In case of a category 2 ISR the prefix "Os_Isr_" is internally added to the function name.

- **作用域**: ECU

- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false

########## 3. OsIsrOsInterruptLockBudget

##### 30. OsIsrTimingProtection

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `dca48f42-bb0b-4cbd-a88d-7c7ea987c7a9`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `OsIsrTimingProtection`
- **多重性**: [0..1]

**容器描述**:

This container contains all parameters which are related to timing protection.

Timing protection is only allowed if OS scalability class is set to SC2 or SC4.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **4** 个参数：

####### FLOAT 类型参数

######## 1. OsIsrAllInterruptLockBudget

- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter contains the maximum time for which the ISR is allowed to lock all interrupts (via SuspendAllInterrupts() or DisableAllInterrupts()) (in seconds).

######## 2. OsIsrExecutionBudget

- **UUID**: `cfea59c1-f455-4192-8b16-f0bf51bacbee`

- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]

- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The parameter contains the maximum allowed execution time of the interrupt (in seconds).

######## 3. OsIsrOsInterruptLockBudget

- **UUID**: `909f93f5-bc0c-4539-9a1e-22291d8f12a0`
- **参数名**: `OsIsrOsInterruptLockBudget`

- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter contains the maximum time for which the ISR is allowed to lock all Category 2 interrupts (via SuspendOSInterrupts()) (in seconds).

######## 4. OsIsrTimeFrame

- **UUID**: `a4da5405-08fc-4c27-80ee-a3b2adad7448`

- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter contains the minimum inter-arrival time between successive interrupts (in seconds).

##### 33. OsMemoryProtection

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `bbdd16af-a0de-46fd-83f9-3acffc6b7c9a`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `OsMemoryProtection`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Container to structure all memory protection relevant configuration parameters.

Memory protection is only allowed if OS scalability class is set to SC3 or SC4.

###### 配置容器

本模块包含 **3** 个配置容器：

####### 1. OsMemoryRegion

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `dde4cc99-a088-4981-a246-488e16da08fa`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `OsMemoryRegion`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Container to structure the configuration parameters of one memory protection region.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **4** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. OsMemoryRegionIdentifier

- **UUID**: `2b6a26ff-3b81-4834-8468-2ccaf02ab6a0`
- **参数名**: `OsMemoryRegionIdentifier`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Defines the optional hardware specific memory protection region identifier.

The interpretation of this parameter is hardware specific (e.g. PID, ProtectionSet or ASID).

########## 2. OsMemoryRegionProtectionUnitSlot

- **UUID**: `ed633055-bd6c-4d9b-9e9c-c421ee4e794d`
- **参数名**: `OsMemoryRegionProtectionUnitSlot`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This optional parameter specifies on which MPU slot the memory protection region shall be used.

The interpretation of this parameter is hardware specific.

######### STRING 类型参数

########## 1. OsMemoryRegionStartAddress

- **UUID**: `ac84b2d8-71bb-4117-8bd1-e0ab297e18de`
- **参数名**: `OsMemoryRegionStartAddress`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Start address of a memory protection region. The address can be specified either as a valid decimal (1024) or hexadecimal (0x4000) value, or as a valid linker symbol.

StartAddress <= Memory to be accessed

########## 2. OsMemoryRegionEndAddress

- **UUID**: `4294d63a-487a-403f-92a1-b13b7bb6ddd2`
- **参数名**: `OsMemoryRegionEndAddress`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: If a core MPU is used:
Size of a memory protection region. The size can be specified either as a valid decimal (16384) or hexadecimal (0x4000) value.
The value is bound to be a power of 2.
If a system MPU is used:
End address of a memory protection region. The address can be specified either as a valid decimal (1024) or hexadecimal (0x4000) value, or as a valid linker symbol.
Memory to be accessed <= EndAddress.

######## 引用关系

此容器包含 **5** 个引用：

######### REFERENCE 类型引用

########## 1. OsMemoryRegionAccessRights

- **UUID**: `1c9a0ec7-7674-4644-bf32-306028bb2f6f`
- **引用名**: `OsMemoryRegionAccessRights`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [1..infinite]
- **目标**: `OsMemoryRegionAccess`
- **完整路径**: `/MICROSAR/Os/OsPublishedInformation/OsDerivativeInformation/OsMemoryRegionSpecifics/OsMemoryRegionAccess`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This reference is used to set access rights for the memory protection region.

########## 2. OsMemoryRegionFlags

- **UUID**: `f555379d-5713-4b23-9f35-943c9c870622`
- **引用名**: `OsMemoryRegionFlags`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **目标**: `OsMemoryRegionFlag`
- **完整路径**: `/MICROSAR/Os/OsPublishedInformation/OsDerivativeInformation/OsMemoryRegionSpecifics/OsMemoryRegionFlag`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: References hardware specific region flags to be active for this memory protection region.

########## 3. OsMemoryRegionBusMasters

- **UUID**: `a98ede89-30b9-4748-abe1-45fa607a7f73`
- **引用名**: `OsMemoryRegionBusMasters`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **目标**: `OsMemoryRegionBusMaster`
- **完整路径**: `/MICROSAR/Os/OsPublishedInformation/OsDerivativeInformation/OsMemoryRegionSpecifics/OsMemoryRegionBusMaster`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: References each bus master for which the memory protection region shall be valid for.

######### CHOICE-REFERENCE 类型引用

########## 1. OsMemoryRegionOwner

- **UUID**: `01565914-4ede-4bd4-8218-8207a4fdeeb7`
- **引用名**: `OsMemoryRegionOwner`
- **引用类型**: CHOICE-REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **目标引用列表**:
  - `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/Os/OsApplication`
  - `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/Os/OsIsr`
  - `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/Os/OsTask`
- **描述**: Specifies whether a memory protection region is OS-Application, Task or ISR specific or Global.

Application specific:
Add reference to an OsApplication, so the region gets active everytime the configured OS-Application is entered.

Task specific:
Add reference to an OsTask, so the region gets active everytime the configured Task is entered.

ISR specific:
Add reference to a category 2 OsIsr, so the region gets active everytime the configured ISR is entered.

Global:
If no owner (reference) is configured, the region is always active.

########## 2. OsMemoryRegionProtectionUnit

- **UUID**: `aafe1362-192d-4005-b942-ecfdd20c0b5a`
- **引用名**: `OsMemoryRegionProtectionUnit`
- **引用类型**: CHOICE-REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **目标引用列表**:
  - `/MICROSAR/Os/OsPublishedInformation/OsDerivativeInformation/OsSystemMpu`
  - `/MICROSAR/Os/OsPublishedInformation/OsDerivativeInformation/OsPhysicalCore/OsPhysicalCoreMpu`
- **描述**: References on which memory protection unit (core or system) the memory protection region shall be used.

####### 2. OsAccessCheckRegion

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `f9f22212-5e60-4a00-9775-be43f9565eef`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER

- **多重性**: [0..infinite]

**容器描述**:

Container to structure the configuration parameters of one check memory access region.

These regions are used for the AUTOSAR Check[Task|ISR]MemoryAccess() APIs.

######## 参数配置

- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Start address of a check memory access region. The address can be specified either as a valid decimal (1024) or hexadecimal (0x4000) value, or as a valid linker symbol.

StartAddress <= Memory to be accessed

########## 2. OsAccessCheckRegionEndAddress

- **UUID**: `1572a45e-b335-4196-b6c1-eaabfd16e376`
- **参数名**: `OsAccessCheckRegionEndAddress`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: End address of a check memory access region. The address can be specified either as a valid decimal (1024) or hexadecimal (0x4000) value, or as a valid linker symbol.

Memory to be accessed <= EndAddress

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. OsAccessCheckRegionAccessRights

- **UUID**: `51a21e47-9ab4-4129-9f2c-40951325b673`
- **参数名**: `OsAccessCheckRegionAccessRights`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..4]
- **默认值**: `READ`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `READ` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `WRITE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `EXECUTE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `STACK` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter specifies the expected access rights for a check memory access region.
It is possible to combine access types.

READ: Read access right is granted
WRITE: Write access right is granted
EXECUTE: Execution right is granted
STACK: It is a stack region

If nothing selected, no access rights are granted.

  - `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/Os/OsIsr`
  - `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/Os/OsTask`
- **描述**: Specifies the owners of the check memory access region.

Memory to be accessed <= EndAddress.

####### CHOICE-REFERENCE 类型引用

######## 1. OsMemoryRegionOwner

- **UUID**: `01565914-4ede-4bd4-8218-8207a4fdeeb7`
- **引用名**: `OsMemoryRegionOwner`
- **引用类型**: CHOICE-REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **目标引用列表**:
  - `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/Os/OsApplication`
  - `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/Os/OsIsr`
  - `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/Os/OsTask`
- **描述**: Specifies whether a memory protection region is OS-Application, Task or ISR specific or Global.

Application specific:
Add reference to an OsApplication, so the region gets active everytime the configured OS-Application is entered.

Task specific:
Add reference to an OsTask, so the region gets active everytime the configured Task is entered.

ISR specific:
Add reference to a category 2 OsIsr, so the region gets active everytime the configured ISR is entered.

Global:
If no owner (reference) is configured, the region is always active.

  - `/MICROSAR/Os/OsPublishedInformation/OsDerivativeInformation/OsPhysicalCore/OsPhysicalCoreMpu`
- **描述**: References on which memory protection unit (core or system) the memory protection region shall be used.

- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The OsUseResScheduler attribute defines whether the resource RES_SCHEDULER is used within the application.

Note: For multi-core systems the RES_SCHEDULER is generated for each core with name 'RES_SCHEDULER_<CoreName>'.

######## 5. OsForcibleTermination

- **UUID**: `1097e72b-4751-4690-84b9-955638895bb2`
- **参数名**: `OsForcibleTermination`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]

- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter specifies whether forcible termination (killing) is enabled or not.

######## 6. OsGenerateCalloutStubs

- **UUID**: `e54295db-5df8-4554-b80d-ee5c7ccb5a8f`
- **参数名**: `OsGenerateCalloutStubs`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Enable user callout function stub generation for system and OS-Application specific hooks.

true: User callout function stubs will be generated into file Os_Callout_Stubs.c
false: No callout stubs will be generated

######## 7. OsGenerateMemMapForNearAddressing

- **UUID**: `b1803c64-c390-4253-8e07-6d17a2ea05a2`
- **参数名**: `OsGenerateMemMapForNearAddressing`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter specifies if additional memory sections for near addressing mode (e.g. sdata, sbss, sconst) shall be generated into Os_MemMap.h or not. This feature could be used to decrease compile time if memory sections for near addressing mode are not necessary according to the used compiler options. 

true: Generate additional memory sections for near addressing into Os_MemMap.h
false: No memory sections for near addressing will be generated

######## 8. OsGenerateMemMapForThreads

- **UUID**: `8b8f5f31-db61-4fe3-9c84-d8f0f0495c78`
- **参数名**: `OsGenerateMemMapForThreads`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter specifies if additional memory sections for threads (Tasks and ISRs) shall be generated into Os_MemMap.h or not. This feature could be used to decrease compile time if memory sections for threads are not necessary.

true: Generate additional memory sections for threads into Os_MemMap.h
false: No memory sections for threads will be generated

######## 9. OsServiceProtection

- **UUID**: `989672fe-6ed4-4403-8fe4-799b1d8048ac`
- **参数名**: `OsServiceProtection`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Enables service checks specified by AUTOSAR.

######## 10. OsStackUsageMeasurement

- **UUID**: `80c5b1f6-57a3-42b5-bae4-b33bb1102042`
- **参数名**: `OsStackUsageMeasurement`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Enables the MICROSAR OS feature to measure stack usage during runtime. There are several API functions (Os_Get*StackUsage) which can be used by the application to determine the current stack usage. Please refer to the TechnicalReference to see all available stack usage API functions and how to use this feature.

This feature will increase OS start-up time because all configured stacks have to be filled with a check pattern.

######## 11. OsUseXSignalAsyncApiCalls

- **UUID**: `17a6e710-bc3c-45fa-9575-7737ae1df8b9`
- **参数名**: `OsUseXSignalAsyncApiCalls`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter is used to specify if cross-core API calls (XSignal) shall be performed synchronous (false) or asynchronous (true).

It is only considered if at least one valid /MICROSAR/Os/OsCore/OsCoreXSignalChannel is configured.

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. OsScalabilityClass

- **UUID**: `7713ab21-8f7c-47a9-b596-233fc6562cf3`
- **参数名**: `OsScalabilityClass`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `SC1`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `SC1` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `SC2` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `SC3` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `SC4` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **描述**: A scalability class for each System Object "OS" has to be selected. In order to customize the operating system to the needs of the user and to take full advantage of the processor features the operating system can be scaled according to the scalability classes.

SC1: OSEK compliant with AUTOSAR extensions
SC2: Timing Protection
SC3: Memory Protection
SC4: Memory and Timing Protection

######## 2. OsStatus

- **UUID**: `0b1d27b8-3bb3-4dfd-9afd-b466e3fc12b2`
- **参数名**: `OsStatus`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `EXTENDED`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `EXTENDED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `STANDARD` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **描述**: This parameter specifies if the system shall use standard or extended status checks.

######## 3. OsGenerateMemMap

- **UUID**: `2a235dfb-db9c-4bf6-b2cd-e65edb508fd8`
- **参数名**: `OsGenerateMemMap`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `COMPLETE`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `COMPLETE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `USERCODE_AND_STACKS_GROUPED_PER_CORE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `EMPTY` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter defines if complete, reduced or empty AUTOSAR MemMap files (Os_MemMap*.h) are generated.

Note: The parameter /Os/OsOS/OsGenerateMemMapForThreads could also be used to reduce compile time or number of user defined sections.

COMPLETE:
Generate complete MemMap files including compiler specific pragmas (recommended for systems with memory protection).

USERCODE_AND_STACKS_GROUPED_PER_CORE:
Same as COMPLETE but some sections are grouped to decrease number of user defined linker sections:
  - User callout code sections (Tasks, ISRs, Hooks, etc.) of a core are grouped into one user code section
  - All stack sections of a core are grouped into one stack data section
If selected and memory protection is enabled, all configured stack sizes must match the MPU granularity and alignment.
Otherwise stack protection cannot be ensured.

EMPTY:
Generate empty MemMap files to reduce compile time. If selected, the AUTOSAR memory mapping mechanism cannot be used.

######## 4. OsFpuUsage

- **UUID**: `d7b76753-8901-4d61-994b-e7f946f9c6e4`
- **参数名**: `OsFpuUsage`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `NONE`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NONE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ALL` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `INDIVIDUAL` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter is used to specify the usage of the FPU unit, if it is available on the microcontroller. Using the FPU unit typically increases the size of the register context, which is saved and restored during a context switch.

NONE: No thread uses the FPU unit.
ALL: All the threads use the FPU unit.
INDIVIDUAL: The usage is set individual for each thread.

This parameter only takes effect on platforms, where the FPU context is great. On other platforms, the FPU context is always saved. Please refer to the Technical Reference for more detailed information about the concrete platforms.

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. OsORTIDebugSupport

- **UUID**: `25b3fe91-b5f7-4f24-b68d-f3a2fe96f8c7`
- **参数名**: `OsORTIDebugSupport`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `ORTI_22_ADDITIONAL`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ORTI_22_STANDARD` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ORTI_22_ADDITIONAL` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ORTI_23_STANDARD` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ORTI_23_ADDITIONAL` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter specifies if ORTI debug information is generated or not.

ORTI_22_*: Support of standard ORTI 2.2 - one file for each AUTOSAR core is created (Os_Trace_<CoreName>.ORT)
ORTI_23_*: Support of ORTI 2.2 inclusive SMP proposals (multi-core support) - single file (Os_Trace.ORT)

*_STANDARD: Enables ORTI with tracing of Tasks and Isrs -- causes a small offset in runtime and memory consumption compared to non ORTI.
*_ADDITIONAL: Same as *_STANDARD plus Service tracing -- causes a bigger offset in runtime and memory consumption than *_STANDARD.

If nothing is selected (Parameter does not exist), no ORTI debug file is created.

####### 3. OsStackSummary

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `d35a13c9-22d3-4d68-a248-cb489d2e0f58`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `OsStackSummary`
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container provides an overview of all internal calculated OS stacks.
For example it can be used to determine which task uses which stack and how the size is configured.
The size has to be configured at the stack size parameter of the container which is referenced as user.

Note: This stack summary is automatically created and updated during configuration. Manual configuration of stacks in this summary is not supported.

######### 1. OsStack

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `035d8982-3ed1-4b39-b768-387e4a461591`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `OsStack`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container represents an OS stack.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. OsStackSize

- **UUID**: `3a28579e-59ad-434e-8032-b634d32aa287`
- **参数名**: `OsStackSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1024`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the size of the stack in bytes.

Note: For shared stacks the biggest configured stack size is used to set up this value.

########### ENUMERATION 类型参数

############ 1. OsStackType

- **UUID**: `a532c930-5107-46be-bcb0-c8c0991dc06f`
- **参数名**: `OsStackType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `CORE_KERNEL_STACK`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `CORE_CALLBACK_STACK` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `CORE_ERROR_STACK` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `CORE_INIT_STACK` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `CORE_KERNEL_STACK` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `CORE_PROTECTION_STACK` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `CORE_STARTUP_STACK` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `CORE_SHUTDOWN_STACK` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ISR_SHARED_CORE_STACK` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ISR_SHARED_LEVEL_STACK` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `NTF_CALLER_STACK` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `TASK_EXTENDED_STACK` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `TASK_SHARED_INTRES_STACK` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `TASK_SHARED_NONPRE_STACK` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `TASK_SHARED_PRIORITY_STACK` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter defines the type of the stack.

Note: Each type refers to users assigned to the same core.

CORE_CALLBACK_STACK: This stack is shared by all IOC receiver PullCBs.
CORE_ERROR_STACK: This stack is shared by the global and all OS-Application specific ErrorHooks.
CORE_INIT_STACK: This stack is used by the OS initialization.
CORE_KERNEL_STACK: This stack is used by the memory exception handling.
CORE_PROTECTION_STACK: This stack is used by the global ProtectionHook.
CORE_STARTUP_STACK: This stack is shared by the global and all OS-Application specific StartupHooks.
CORE_SHUTDOWN_STACK: This stack is shared by the global and all OS-Application specific ShutdownHooks.

ISR_SHARED_CORE_STACK: This stack is shared by all non-nestable category 2 ISRs.
ISR_SHARED_LEVEL_STACK: This stack is shared by all nestable category 2 ISRs configured to the same interrupt priority level.

NTF_CALLER_STACK: This stack is used by exactly one caller of a non-trusted function.

TASK_EXTENDED_STACK: This stack is only used by the extended user Task.
TASK_SHARED_INTRES_STACK: This stack is shared by all basic Tasks with active stack sharing and the same internal resouce.
TASK_SHARED_NONPRE_STACK: This stack is shared by all non-preemptable basic Tasks with active stack sharing.
TASK_SHARED_PRIORITY_STACK: This stack is shared by all basic Tasks with the same priority.

此容器包含 **2** 个引用：

**容器描述**:

Container to structure all hooks belonging to the OS.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **7** 个参数：

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. OsErrorHook

- **UUID**: `68c4b4f3-061a-4d51-a21e-9ae3fca4137e`
- **参数名**: `OsErrorHook`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: If selected, the ErrorHook routine is called, if an API function returns with an error code unequal to E_OK. It is also called if OS internal errors are detected.

The function ErrorHook has to be provided by the application.

######## 2. OsPostTaskHook

- **UUID**: `829dbbd2-72a7-4d1a-aa1d-45861e031768`
- **参数名**: `OsPostTaskHook`

- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: If selected, the PostTaskHook routine will be called any time the operating system interrupts or terminates a task.

The function PostTaskHook has to be provided by the application.

######## 3. OsPreTaskHook

- **UUID**: `d83fc560-745d-4ee6-829b-3ad2f8b45cf1`

- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: If selected, the PreTaskHook routine will be called any time the operating system starts or resumes a task.

The function PreTaskHook has to be provided by the application.

######## 4. OsProtectionHook

- **UUID**: `d3b32a14-6bcd-41b5-9ed3-f8c5e24432eb`

######## 5. OsShutdownHook

######## 6. OsStartupHook

- **UUID**: `7c427aa5-5113-48c1-b4b1-eac057aa69b2`

- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: If selected, the StartupHook routine will be called by the operating system in the startup phase (after calling StartOS and before starting the first task).

The function StartupHook has to be provided by the application.

######## 7. OsPanicHook

- **UUID**: `fb94130e-6590-42be-9f64-4cc3520131f6`

- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: If selected, the PanicHook routine will be called by the operating system if an inconsistent OS state is detected and regular shutdown cannot be reached.

The function Os_PanicHook has to be provided by the application.

##### 40. OsStackSummary

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `d35a13c9-22d3-4d68-a248-cb489d2e0f58`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `OsStackSummary`
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container provides an overview of all internal calculated OS stacks.
For example it can be used to determine which task uses which stack and how the size is configured.
The size has to be configured at the stack size parameter of the container which is referenced as user.

Note: This stack summary is automatically created and updated during configuration. Manual configuration of stacks in this summary is not supported.

###### 配置容器

本模块包含 **1** 个配置容器：

####### 1. OsStack

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `035d8982-3ed1-4b39-b768-387e4a461591`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `OsStack`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container represents an OS stack.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. OsStackSize

- **UUID**: `3a28579e-59ad-434e-8032-b634d32aa287`
- **参数名**: `OsStackSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1024`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the size of the stack in bytes.

Note: For shared stacks the biggest configured stack size is used to set up this value.

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. OsStackType

- **UUID**: `a532c930-5107-46be-bcb0-c8c0991dc06f`
- **参数名**: `OsStackType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `CORE_KERNEL_STACK`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `CORE_CALLBACK_STACK` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `CORE_ERROR_STACK` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `CORE_INIT_STACK` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `CORE_KERNEL_STACK` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `CORE_PROTECTION_STACK` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `CORE_STARTUP_STACK` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `CORE_SHUTDOWN_STACK` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ISR_SHARED_CORE_STACK` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ISR_SHARED_LEVEL_STACK` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `NTF_CALLER_STACK` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `TASK_EXTENDED_STACK` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `TASK_SHARED_INTRES_STACK` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `TASK_SHARED_NONPRE_STACK` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `TASK_SHARED_PRIORITY_STACK` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter defines the type of the stack.

Note: Each type refers to users assigned to the same core.

CORE_CALLBACK_STACK: This stack is shared by all IOC receiver PullCBs.
CORE_ERROR_STACK: This stack is shared by the global and all OS-Application specific ErrorHooks.
CORE_INIT_STACK: This stack is used by the OS initialization.
CORE_KERNEL_STACK: This stack is used by the memory exception handling.
CORE_PROTECTION_STACK: This stack is used by the global ProtectionHook.
CORE_STARTUP_STACK: This stack is shared by the global and all OS-Application specific StartupHooks.
CORE_SHUTDOWN_STACK: This stack is shared by the global and all OS-Application specific ShutdownHooks.

ISR_SHARED_CORE_STACK: This stack is shared by all non-nestable category 2 ISRs.
ISR_SHARED_LEVEL_STACK: This stack is shared by all nestable category 2 ISRs configured to the same interrupt priority level.

NTF_CALLER_STACK: This stack is used by exactly one caller of a non-trusted function.

TASK_EXTENDED_STACK: This stack is only used by the extended user Task.
TASK_SHARED_INTRES_STACK: This stack is shared by all basic Tasks with active stack sharing and the same internal resouce.
TASK_SHARED_NONPRE_STACK: This stack is shared by all non-preemptable basic Tasks with active stack sharing.
TASK_SHARED_PRIORITY_STACK: This stack is shared by all basic Tasks with the same priority.

此容器包含 **2** 个引用：

Contains all derivative specific information needed by the OS generator.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **7** 个参数：

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. OsMpuEndAddressAccessible

- **UUID**: `ce9f1d09-6612-4933-8381-e72a7d5a8c4b`
- **参数名**: `OsMpuEndAddressAccessible`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Defines whether the configured memory region end address is accessible or not.

This depends on the hardware MPU evaluation of the effective address (EA):
StartAddress <= EA <= EndAddress: True
StartAddress <= EA  <  EndAddress: False

- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Defines whether the core is allowed to start the master core. Note that at most one physical core can be configured with this flag equal to True. Note that when this parameter is set to True, this core must be an auto-start core.

############ 3. OsPhysicalCorePrivelegedHwAccess

- **UUID**: `a3504af3-f7ef-4a4e-b70a-c42e0d2ee8b2`
- **参数名**: `OsPhysicalCorePrivelegedHwAccess`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Defines whether the core has the privilege to initialize peripheral registers, e.g.: interrupt source registers in system interrupt controllers.

此容器包含 **3** 个参数：

############# INTEGER 类型参数

- **UUID**: `f977b740-271e-4222-a9e7-1a4bddf3ca89`
- **参数名**: `OsPhysicalCoreIntCtrlMaxSources`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `999`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter specifies the highest configurable interrupt source.

Example: If the interrupt controller supports 1024 interrupt sources (0-1023), the highest configurable interrupt source is 1023.

############## 2. OsPhysicalCoreIntCtrlMaxPrios

- **UUID**: `90f61a22-6ef7-4469-8d86-5cc333557066`
- **参数名**: `OsPhysicalCoreIntCtrlMaxPrios`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `255`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter specifies the highest configurable interrupt priority.

Example: If an interrupt controller supports 64 priorities (0-63), the highest configurable priority is 63.

############# ENUMERATION 类型参数

############## 1. OsPhysicalCoreIntCtrlPrioOrder

- **UUID**: `bc6dd608-b15e-4ee4-99ed-7f771520f438`

- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ASC` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DESC` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter is used to describe the interrupt priority interpretation.

ASC: Higher values lead to higher priorities
DESC: Lower values lead to higher priorities

- **需要索引**: false

  - `PIT` (来源: Vector Informatik)

- **描述**: Specifies the counting mode supported by the underlying hardware unit.
FRT: A free running up counting timer.
PIT: A periodic interrupt timer.
FRTPIT: Hardware can be configured as PIT or FRT (e.g. via register flag).

- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter specifies the highest configurable interrupt source.

- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter specifies the highest configurable interrupt priority.

Example: If an interrupt controller supports 64 priorities (0-63), the highest configurable priority is 63.

- **容器名称**: `OsInterruptSupportedType`

- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Defines the derivative specific supported interrupt types.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

########### BOOLEAN 类型参数

############ 1. OsInterruptSupportedTypeSeparateSources

- **UUID**: `0f4fd27e-1ac5-4d0f-a28c-8af74820b6f7`
- **参数名**: `OsInterruptSupportedTypeSeparateSources`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines whether the interrupt type (e.g. FIQ) has separate sources or not.

true: The interrupt type has its own sources.
false: Interrupt sources shall be unique independent from the type.

############ 2. OsInterruptSupportedTypeSeparatePriority

- **UUID**: `a1e6b740-dad7-40ec-9cf0-a6d9c1bc7076`
- **参数名**: `OsInterruptSupportedTypeSeparatePriority`

- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines whether the interrupt type (e.g. DMA) has a separate priority or not.

true: The interrupt type has its own priority.
false: The priority value needs to be validated according to interrupt category settings.

- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Defines whether the core is allowed to start the master core. Note that at most one physical core can be configured with this flag equal to True. Note that when this parameter is set to True, this core must be an auto-start core.

########## 3. OsPhysicalCorePrivelegedHwAccess

- **UUID**: `a3504af3-f7ef-4a4e-b70a-c42e0d2ee8b2`

- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Defines whether the core has the privilege to initialize peripheral registers, e.g.: interrupt source registers in system interrupt controllers.

- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `OsPhysicalCoreIntCtrl`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Container to structure all core interrupt controller attributes.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **3** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. OsPhysicalCoreIntCtrlMaxSources

- **UUID**: `f977b740-271e-4222-a9e7-1a4bddf3ca89`
- **参数名**: `OsPhysicalCoreIntCtrlMaxSources`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `999`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter specifies the highest configurable interrupt source.

Example: If the interrupt controller supports 1024 interrupt sources (0-1023), the highest configurable interrupt source is 1023.

############ 2. OsPhysicalCoreIntCtrlMaxPrios

- **UUID**: `90f61a22-6ef7-4469-8d86-5cc333557066`
- **参数名**: `OsPhysicalCoreIntCtrlMaxPrios`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `255`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter specifies the highest configurable interrupt priority.

Example: If an interrupt controller supports 64 priorities (0-63), the highest configurable priority is 63.

########### ENUMERATION 类型参数

############ 1. OsPhysicalCoreIntCtrlPrioOrder

- **UUID**: `bc6dd608-b15e-4ee4-99ed-7f771520f438`
- **参数名**: `OsPhysicalCoreIntCtrlPrioOrder`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]

- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ASC` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DESC` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter is used to describe the interrupt priority interpretation.

ASC: Higher values lead to higher priorities
DESC: Lower values lead to higher priorities

- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:

  - `PIT` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `FRTPIT` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: Specifies the counting mode supported by the underlying hardware unit.
FRT: A free running up counting timer.
PIT: A periodic interrupt timer.
FRTPIT: Hardware can be configured as PIT or FRT (e.g. via register flag).

- **UUID**: `3e931e5e-87a1-4ad2-8ed8-87cc3449f7f5`

- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `999`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter specifies the highest configurable interrupt source.

Example: If the interrupt controller supports 1024 interrupt sources (0-1023), the highest configurable interrupt source is 1023.

########## 2. OsSystemIntCtrlMaxPrios

- **UUID**: `a7dac556-6bfb-40ca-ad60-aa18b0a0dae9`

- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter specifies the highest configurable interrupt priority.

Example: If an interrupt controller supports 64 priorities (0-63), the highest configurable priority is 63.

DESC: Lower values lead to higher priorities

####### 22. OsInterruptSupportedType

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `1bd3752f-d7df-483c-96f0-60ebc8378809`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `OsInterruptSupportedType`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Defines the derivative specific supported interrupt types.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. OsInterruptSupportedTypeSeparateSources

- **UUID**: `0f4fd27e-1ac5-4d0f-a28c-8af74820b6f7`
- **参数名**: `OsInterruptSupportedTypeSeparateSources`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines whether the interrupt type (e.g. FIQ) has separate sources or not.

true: The interrupt type has its own sources.
false: Interrupt sources shall be unique independent from the type.

########## 2. OsInterruptSupportedTypeSeparatePriority

- **UUID**: `a1e6b740-dad7-40ec-9cf0-a6d9c1bc7076`
- **参数名**: `OsInterruptSupportedTypeSeparatePriority`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines whether the interrupt type (e.g. DMA) has a separate priority or not.

true: The interrupt type has its own priority.
false: The priority value needs to be validated according to interrupt category settings.

##### 43. OsDerivativeInformation

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `74cbb4f9-b60a-40f2-97ea-f951f5411d3c`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `OsDerivativeInformation`
- **多重性**: [1..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Contains all derivative specific information needed by the OS generator.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **7** 个参数：

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. OsMpuEndAddressAccessible

- **UUID**: `ce9f1d09-6612-4933-8381-e72a7d5a8c4b`
- **参数名**: `OsMpuEndAddressAccessible`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Defines whether the configured memory region end address is accessible or not.

This depends on the hardware MPU evaluation of the effective address (EA):
StartAddress <= EA <= EndAddress: True
StartAddress <= EA  <  EndAddress: False

####### STRING 类型参数

######## 1. OsMpuAlignment

- **UUID**: `79eba0e6-de15-4724-8c69-877f945cbed6`
- **参数名**: `OsMpuAlignment`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **字符串变体**:
  - 默认值: `0`
- **描述**: Defines the derivative specific alignment of stack linker sections in bytes.

0: No alignment will be generated
SIZE: Stack sections will be aligned to configured size

######## 2. OsMpuMemoryGapSize

- **UUID**: `f650b0e6-01c7-4703-a575-3e5e7a30583c`
- **参数名**: `OsMpuMemoryGapSize`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **字符串变体**:
  - 默认值: `16`

- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN

- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Defines whether the core is auto started after system reset.

########## 2. OsPhysicalCoreMasterStartAllowed

- **UUID**: `c0ba2c8a-dffa-4e73-82f5-bb2736cb0f20`
- **参数名**: `OsPhysicalCoreMasterStartAllowed`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Defines whether the core is allowed to start the master core. Note that at most one physical core can be configured with this flag equal to True. Note that when this parameter is set to True, this core must be an auto-start core.

- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Defines whether the core has the privilege to initialize peripheral registers, e.g.: interrupt source registers in system interrupt controllers.

Container to structure all core interrupt controller attributes.

此容器包含 **3** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

- **UUID**: `f977b740-271e-4222-a9e7-1a4bddf3ca89`
- **参数名**: `OsPhysicalCoreIntCtrlMaxSources`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `999`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter specifies the highest configurable interrupt source.

Example: If the interrupt controller supports 1024 interrupt sources (0-1023), the highest configurable interrupt source is 1023.

############ 2. OsPhysicalCoreIntCtrlMaxPrios

- **UUID**: `90f61a22-6ef7-4469-8d86-5cc333557066`
- **参数名**: `OsPhysicalCoreIntCtrlMaxPrios`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `255`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter specifies the highest configurable interrupt priority.

Example: If an interrupt controller supports 64 priorities (0-63), the highest configurable priority is 63.

########### ENUMERATION 类型参数

############ 1. OsPhysicalCoreIntCtrlPrioOrder

- **UUID**: `bc6dd608-b15e-4ee4-99ed-7f771520f438`

- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ASC` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DESC` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter is used to describe the interrupt priority interpretation.

ASC: Higher values lead to higher priorities
DESC: Lower values lead to higher priorities

ASC: Higher values lead to higher priorities
DESC: Lower values lead to higher priorities

- **描述**: Specifies the counting mode supported by the underlying hardware unit.
FRT: A free running up counting timer.
PIT: A periodic interrupt timer.
FRTPIT: Hardware can be configured as PIT or FRT (e.g. via register flag).

- **容器名称**: `OsInterruptSupportedType`

**容器描述**:

Defines the derivative specific supported interrupt types.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. OsInterruptSupportedTypeSeparateSources

- **UUID**: `0f4fd27e-1ac5-4d0f-a28c-8af74820b6f7`
- **参数名**: `OsInterruptSupportedTypeSeparateSources`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines whether the interrupt type (e.g. FIQ) has separate sources or not.

true: The interrupt type has its own sources.
false: Interrupt sources shall be unique independent from the type.

########## 2. OsInterruptSupportedTypeSeparatePriority

- **UUID**: `a1e6b740-dad7-40ec-9cf0-a6d9c1bc7076`
- **参数名**: `OsInterruptSupportedTypeSeparatePriority`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines whether the interrupt type (e.g. DMA) has a separate priority or not.

true: The interrupt type has its own priority.
false: The priority value needs to be validated according to interrupt category settings.

- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Defines whether the core is allowed to start the master core. Note that at most one physical core can be configured with this flag equal to True. Note that when this parameter is set to True, this core must be an auto-start core.

########## 2. OsPhysicalCoreIntCtrlMaxPrios

- **参数名**: `OsPhysicalCoreIntCtrlMaxPrios`
