- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter specifies the highest configurable interrupt priority.

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. OsPhysicalCoreIntCtrlPrioOrder

- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:

  - `DESC` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter is used to describe the interrupt priority interpretation.

ASC: Higher values lead to higher priorities
DESC: Lower values lead to higher priorities

  - `ASC` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DESC` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter is used to describe the interrupt priority interpretation.

ASC: Higher values lead to higher priorities
DESC: Lower values lead to higher priorities

- **描述**: Specifies the counting mode supported by the underlying hardware unit.
FRT: A free running up counting timer.
PIT: A periodic interrupt timer.
FRTPIT: Hardware can be configured as PIT or FRT (e.g. via register flag).

- **容器名称**: `OsInterruptSupportedType`

Defines the derivative specific supported interrupt types.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. OsInterruptSupportedTypeSeparateSources

- **UUID**: `0f4fd27e-1ac5-4d0f-a28c-8af74820b6f7`
- **参数名**: `OsInterruptSupportedTypeSeparateSources`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines whether the interrupt type (e.g. FIQ) has separate sources or not.

false: Interrupt sources shall be unique independent from the type.

- **UUID**: `a1e6b740-dad7-40ec-9cf0-a6d9c1bc7076`
- **参数名**: `OsInterruptSupportedTypeSeparatePriority`

- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines whether the interrupt type (e.g. DMA) has a separate priority or not.

true: The interrupt type has its own priority.
false: The priority value needs to be validated according to interrupt category settings.

##### 66. OsScheduleTable

**容器属性**:

- **容器名称**: `OsScheduleTable`
- **多重性**: [0..65534]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

An OsScheduleTable addresses the synchronization issue by providing an encapsulation of a statically defined set of alarms that cannot be modified at runtime.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the modulus of the schedule table (in ticks).

######## 1. OsScheduleTableRepeating

- **UUID**: `7082fcab-f3e1-4c8d-954a-e69adb91900b`

- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN

- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: true: First expiry point on the schedule table shall be processed at final expiry point delay ticks after the final expiry point is processed.
false: The schedule table processing stops when the final expiry point is processed.

##### 73. OsSpinlock

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `eb35faf5-f995-4804-a592-f7dc36d73f71`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `OsSpinlock`
- **多重性**: [0..65534]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

An OsSpinlock object is used to co-ordinate concurrent access by tasks and category 2 ISRs on different cores to a shared resource.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. OsSpinlockLockMethod

- **UUID**: `57176593-899e-44f9-8130-bf6187c29eac`
- **参数名**: `OsSpinlockLockMethod`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `LOCK_CAT2_INTERRUPTS`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `LOCK_ALL_INTERRUPTS` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `LOCK_CAT2_INTERRUPTS` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `LOCK_NOTHING` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `LOCK_WITH_RES_SCHEDULER` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **描述**: Lock method which is used when a spinlock is taken. Note that it is possible that a user (e.g. a Task) might hold more than one spinlock. In this case the last lock taken is forced to use at least a lock methode which locks as strong as the current one.

######## 2. OsSpinlockLockType

- **UUID**: `4e972f35-fb5a-48e2-b84c-dc1a8417efb6`
- **参数名**: `OsSpinlockLockType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `STANDARD`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `STANDARD` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `OPTIMIZED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: Allows to select an optimized fast way for spinlock administration.

STANDARD: Standard AUTOSAR implementation
OPTIMIZED: Fast optimized MICROSAR OS implementation

###### 引用关系

此容器包含 **2** 个引用：

####### REFERENCE 类型引用

######## 1. OsSpinlockAccessingApplication

- **UUID**: `3391f40c-f4ea-49ca-a99c-91761ee2ac0d`
- **引用名**: `OsSpinlockAccessingApplication`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [1..infinite]
- **目标**: `OsApplication`
- **完整路径**: `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/Os/OsApplication`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Reference to OS-Applications that have an access to this object.

######## 2. OsSpinlockSuccessor

- **UUID**: `9b42c6ad-166a-4b89-91c4-7bf22accb8f2`
- **引用名**: `OsSpinlockSuccessor`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **目标**: `OsSpinlock`
- **完整路径**: `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/Os/OsSpinlock`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: To check whether a spinlock can be occupied (in a nested way) without any danger of deadlock, a linked list of spinlocks can be defined. A spinlock can only be occupied in the order of the linked list. It is allowed to skip a spinlock. If no linked list is specified, spinlocks cannot be nested.

##### 74. OsTask

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `d02c28d3-5f1c-4f5c-92ed-27231538ca6b`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `OsTask`
- **多重性**: [0..65534]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container represents a task.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **8** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. OsTaskActivation

- **UUID**: `d2f08f84-9c00-40f4-b630-16a0df3369ce`
- **参数名**: `OsTaskActivation`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This attribute defines the maximum number of queued activation requests for the task. A value equal to "1" means that at any time only a single activation is permitted for this task. Note that the value must be a natural number starting at 1.

######## 2. OsTaskPriority

- **UUID**: `d62a4f78-7075-4fd1-83fb-d1ff443c42ab`
- **参数名**: `OsTaskPriority`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The priority of a task is defined by the value of this attribute. This value has to be understood as a relative value, i.e. the values show only the relative ordering of the tasks.

Note: The generator has a special treatment for the idle task. The idle task has the virtual priority 0xFFFFFFFF to differentiate it from regular tasks. It will be generated to have the lowest priority, even if there are tasks configured with priority 0.

######## 3. OsTaskMemoryProtectionIdentifier

- **UUID**: `8c47dbcf-ec31-4143-be53-ee4e5355c700`
- **参数名**: `OsTaskMemoryProtectionIdentifier`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This optional parameter can be used to specify a memory protection identifier for the task.

If this parameter is not set but the owner OS-Application has a memory protection identifier specified this value will be used for the task.

######## 4. OsTaskStackSize

- **UUID**: `0dbcbecb-62cf-4630-bd33-60d22fdeee92`
- **参数名**: `OsTaskStackSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1024`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This attribute specifies the stack size of the task in bytes.

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. OsTaskStackSharing

- **UUID**: `93573a1c-7b60-4dc0-8c60-6d8c4eb6eaba`
- **参数名**: `OsTaskStackSharing`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Specifies whether the task shall share a stack with other basic tasks or not.
Basic tasks with same priority always share a stack independently from this parameter.
For detailed information about task stack sharing please refer to the TechnicalReference_Os.pdf chapter "Task Stack Sharing".

true: The task is enabled for additional stack sharing.
false: The task is not marked for additional stack sharing.

Attention:
Basic tasks which have this attribute set to TRUE must never call the OS service Schedule().

Note:
For shared stacks the biggest configured size will be used to set up the stack size.

######## 2. OsTaskUsesFpu

- **UUID**: `b765d06f-cb5b-4014-b89c-23bffcce7367`
- **参数名**: `OsTaskUsesFpu`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter is used to specify if this task uses the FPU unit.

This parameter only takes effect, if the parameter /MICROSAR/Os/OsOS/OsFpuUsage is set to "INDIVIDUAL".

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. OsTaskSchedule

- **UUID**: `7d85e2e8-128b-46b4-8d7a-8940474c7379`
- **参数名**: `OsTaskSchedule`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `FULL`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `FULL` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `NON` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **描述**: The OsTaskSchedule attribute defines the preemptability of the task.

######## 2. OsTaskType

- **UUID**: `2b2d8f62-ce1d-4e8c-8520-08cce0c5d87b`
- **参数名**: `OsTaskType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `AUTO`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `AUTO` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `BASIC` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `EXTENDED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter specifies the type of task. If AUTO (should be used) the type is calculated automatically from other parameters.

It is possible to preselect the type, if there are restrictions to use a special task type. An error message is created if there are differences between the preselected and the calculated task type.

####### 4. OsTaskRoundRobinScheduling

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `4388f407-cb56-4e48-8763-d446c019ea4e`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `OsTaskRoundRobinScheduling`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container holds the parameter needed for round robin scheduling.
If this container exists for a task, the task is considered to participate in round robin scheduling.
If a task has this container configured, all tasks on the same core with the same priority shall also have this container configured
(So round robin scheduling has either to be used for all tasks of a specific priority or for none of these).

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **1** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. OsTaskNumScheduleEventsRoundRobin

- **UUID**: `59511d92-59ea-4e4c-8803-c4a47edd78b8`

- **数据类型**: INTEGER

- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..4294967295]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Defines the number of TimeSlices this task may consume.

##### 79. OsAlarmAction

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `f29276b6-e4f8-437e-bb97-cf391547c580`
- **容器类型**: CHOICE-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `OsAlarmAction`
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器选择**:

- **OsAlarmActivateTask**
  - UUID: `0f95c602-e982-4e74-86cf-3c858e0c3b1a`
  - 描述: This container specifies the parameters to activate a task.
- **OsAlarmCallback**
  - UUID: `1f2d1dc0-bf29-4653-88d6-d46a4dd0c787`
  - 描述: This container specifies the parameters to call a callback OS alarm action.
- **OsAlarmIncrementCounter**
  - UUID: `93b22862-261e-468a-9cf3-30f04761534c`
  - 描述: This container specifies the parameters to increment a counter.
- **OsAlarmSetEvent**
  - UUID: `a95ee055-db5e-4c16-852d-730689794500`
  - 描述: This container specifies the parameters to set an event.
- **OsAlarmScheduleEventRoundRobin**
  - UUID: `790cb604-ce45-4df5-a4ec-dd4c7215376b`
  - 描述: This container specifies, that the alarm is used for round robin scheduling.
Exactly one alarm with this container is needed per core which has one or more tasks that have round robin scheduling configured by means of the container: OsTaskRoundRobinScheduling.
Typically, this is enforced by a validator with a preferred solving action. So no need to add such an alarm manually.

**容器描述**:

This container defines which type of notification is used when the alarm expires.
